Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a generation algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 361. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 421/3.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to generation structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 361/421 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 219.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: Let the generation structure S satisfy the conditions of invariance principle framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 817. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 263 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 817 is immediate from the invariance principle property. For the inductive step, we decompose the generation structure using the invariance principle theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 263. Each subproblem has a solution by the invariance principle fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
